country_event = {
	id = demindia.1 #intro
	immediate = {log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: event demindia.1"}
 	title = demindia.1.t
 	desc = demindia.1.desc
 	picture = GFX_report_event_india_taj_mahal

	is_triggered_only = yes

 	option = {
 		name = demindia.1.a
 	}
}

 country_event = {
	id = demindia.2 #Commies riot
	immediate = {log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: event demindia.2"}
 	title = demindia.2.t
 	desc = demindia.2.desc
 	picture = GFX_report_event_india_communists

	fire_only_once = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes

 	option = { #Leave it be
 		name = demindia.2.a
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 2
		}
		add_timed_idea = {
			idea = RAJ_strike
			days = 30
		}
		add_popularity = {
			ideology = socialist
			popularity = 0.15
		}
		set_temp_variable = { RAJ_LS_t_party = 5 }
		set_temp_variable = { RAJ_LS_t_state = -1 }
		set_temp_variable = { RAJ_LS_t_amount = 15 }
		RAJ_LS_add_support = yes
		add_popularity = {
			ideology = social_democrat
			popularity = 0.05
		}
		set_temp_variable = { RAJ_LS_t_party = 0 }
		set_temp_variable = { RAJ_LS_t_state = -1 }
		set_temp_variable = { RAJ_LS_t_amount = 5 }
		RAJ_LS_add_support = yes
 	}

	option = { #Crack down
 		name = demindia.2.b
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 1
		}
		add_timed_idea = {
			idea = RAJ_strike
			days = 5
		}
		add_stability = -0.05
		add_popularity = {
			ideology = socialist
			popularity = 0.05
		}
		set_temp_variable = { RAJ_LS_t_party = 5 }
		set_temp_variable = { RAJ_LS_t_state = -1 }
		set_temp_variable = { RAJ_LS_t_amount = 5 }
		RAJ_LS_add_support = yes
 	}
 }

			}
			OR = {

					has_country_flag = RAJ_CSSD_MAR
					has_country_flag = RAJ_CSSD_DEM
				}
				has_country_flag = RAJ_CS_Alone
			}
			OR = {
				has_country_flag = RAJ_INC
				has_country_flag = RAJ_SL_Alone
				has_country_flag = RAJ_SL_LB
			}
			OR = {
				has_country_flag = RAJ_SL_LB
				has_country_flag = RAJ_SC_ML
				AND = {
					has_country_flag = RAJ_SL_Alone
					OR = {
						has_country_flag = RAJ_SC_Alone
						has_country_flag = RAJ_SC_F
						has_country_flag = RAJ_INCSC_Populist
					}
				}
			}
			OR = {
				has_country_flag = RAJ_SC_F
				has_country_flag = RAJ_SC_ML
				has_country_flag = RAJ_SC_Alone
				has_country_flag = RAJ_INCSC_Populist
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = { #Yay

	desc = demindia.427.desc
	picture = GFX_report_event_india_famine

	immediate = {log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: event demindia.427"}

	fire_only_once = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = demindia.427.a
		IF = {
			limit = {
				has_idea = RAJ_famine1
			}
			swap_ideas = {
				remove_idea = RAJ_famine1
				add_idea = RAJ_famine2
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_idea = RAJ_famine2
			}
			swap_ideas = {
				remove_idea = RAJ_famine2
				add_idea = RAJ_famine3
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_idea = RAJ_famine3
			}
			swap_ideas = {
				remove_idea = RAJ_famine3
				add_idea = RAJ_famine4
			}
		}
		else = {
			add_ideas = RAJ_famine1
		}
	}
}

			else = {
			}
		}

					has_country_flag = ger_attending_denuke_conference
					tag = RAJ
				}

				limit = {
					OR = {
						AND = {
							check_variable = {
								var = RAJ_denuke_attendees
								value = 3
								compare = equals
							}
							check_variable = {
								var = RAJ_denuke_yays
								value = 3
								compare = equals
							}
						}
						AND = {
							check_variable = {
								var = RAJ_denuke_attendees
								value = 4
								compare = equals
							}
							check_variable = {
								var = RAJ_denuke_yays
								value = 3
								compare = greater_than_or_equals
							}
						}
					}
				}
				GER = {
					country_event = {
						id = india.467
						days = 15
					}
				}
				JAP = {
					country_event = {
						id = india.467
						days = 15
					}
				}
				USA = {
					country_event = {
						id = india.467
						days = 15
					}
				}
				RAJ = {
					country_event = {
						id = india.467
						days = 15
					}
				}
			}
			else = {
				GER = {
					country_event = {
						id = india.468
						days = 15
					}
				}
				JAP = {
					country_event = {
						id = india.468
						days = 15
					}
				}

								var = RAJ_denuke_attendees
								value = 3
								compare = equals
							}
							check_variable = {
								var = RAJ_denuke_yays
								value = 3
								compare = equals
							}
						}
						AND = {
							check_variable = {
								var = RAJ_denuke_attendees
								value = 4
								compare = equals
							}
							check_variable = {
								var = RAJ_denuke_yays
								value = 3
								compare = greater_than_or_equals
							}
						}
					}
				}
				GER = {
					country_event = {
						id = india.467
						days = 15
					}
				}
				JAP = {
					country_event = {
						id = india.467
						days = 15
					}
				}
				USA = {
					country_event = {
						id = india.467
						days = 15
					}
				}
				RAJ = {
					country_event = {
						id = india.467
						days = 15
					}
				}
			}
			else = {
				GER = {
					country_event = {
						id = india.468
						days = 15
					}
				}
				JAP = {
					country_event = {
						id = india.468
						days = 15
					}
				}
				USA = {
					country_event = {
						id = india.468
						days = 15
					}
				}
				RAJ = {
					country_event = {
						id = india.468
						days = 15
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

			}

				ideology = despotism

			}

				ideology = social_conservative
				popularity = 0.20
			}
			set_temp_variable = { RAJ_LS_t_party = 4 }
			set_temp_variable = { RAJ_LS_t_state = -1 }
			set_temp_variable = { RAJ_LS_t_amount = 20 }
			RAJ_LS_add_support = yes
			add_popularity = {
				ideology = social_democrat
				popularity = 0.10
			}
			set_temp_variable = { RAJ_LS_t_party = 0 }
			set_temp_variable = { RAJ_LS_t_state = -1 }

			RAJ_LS_add_support = yes

				name = "Supreme Commander Brij Mohan Kaul"

				ideology = despotism_subtype

				}
			}
		}

country_event = {
	id = demindiaforeign.1 #Naval Deal
	title = demindiaforeign.1.t
	desc = demindiaforeign.1.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_generic_sign_treaty1

	immediate = {log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: event demindiaforeign.1"}

	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes

	option = { #yeet lets go boys
		name = demindiaforeign.1.a
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 10
		}
		RAJ = {
			if = { #Germany
				limit = {
					has_completed_focus = RAJ_Diplo_Germany
				}
				add_opinion_modifier = {
					target = GER
					modifier = RAJ_Naval_Glucose_Guardian
				}
			}
			if = { #Italy
				limit = {
					has_completed_focus = RAJ_Diplo_Italy
				}
				add_opinion_modifier = {
					target = ITA
					modifier = RAJ_Naval_Glucose_Guardian
				}
			}
			if = { #Japan
				limit = {
					has_completed_focus = RAJ_Diplo_Japan
				}
				add_opinion_modifier = {
					target = JAP
					modifier = RAJ_Naval_Glucose_Guardian
				}
			}
			if = { #USA
				limit = {
					has_completed_focus = RAJ_Diplo_US
				}
				add_opinion_modifier = {
					target = USA
					modifier = RAJ_Naval_Glucose_Guardian
				}
			}
			country_event = {
				id = demindiaforeign.2
				days = 10
			}
		}
	}
	option = { #I dont care about India
		name = demindiaforeign.1.b
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 1
		}
		RAJ = {
			if = { #Germany
				limit = {
					has_completed_focus = RAJ_Diplo_Germany
				}
				add_opinion_modifier = {
					target = GER
					modifier = RAJ_No_Naval_Trade
				}
			}
			if = { #Italy
				limit = {
					has_completed_focus = RAJ_Diplo_Italy
				}
				add_opinion_modifier = {
					target = ITA
					modifier = RAJ_No_Naval_Trade
				}
			}
			if = { #Japan
				limit = {
					has_completed_focus = RAJ_Diplo_Japan
				}
				add_opinion_modifier = {
					target = JAP
					modifier = RAJ_No_Naval_Trade
				}
			}
			if = { #USA
